Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The bayesian_statistics operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 906. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 139 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_906(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 906. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^139(S, F) is trivial. QED.
